1891.] TIENTSIN. 9 (Received 24th October 1891J CUSTOM HOUSE, TIENTSIN, 15th October 1891. SIR, I.&mdash;In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the third quarter of the year 1891. Remarks.&mdash;During July the highest reading of the barometer was 30.080, on the 23rd; and the lowest 29.380, on the 19th. The highest temperature was 990, on the 18th; and the lowest 720, on the 4th. S.E. winds prevailed. Rain fell on 10 days. During August the highest reading of the barometer was 30.078, on the 19th; and the lowest 29.600, on the 3rd. The highest temperature was 98&deg;, on the 2nd and 23rd; and the lowest 730, on the 10th. S.E. winds prevailed. Rain fell on 13 days. During September the highest reading of the barometer was 30.150, on the 30th; and the lowest 29.740, on the 1st. The highest temperature was 96&deg;, on the 9th and 10th; and the lowest 70&deg;, on the 30th. S.E. winds prevailed. Rain fell on 5 days. IX. 1891.] lappa. 197 (Received i yth October 1891.) CUSTOM HOUSE, LAPPA, ioth October 1891. SIR, i.&mdash;a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the third quarter of the year 1891, July-September. b. The Custom House ($&pound; &sect;j&sect;) was opened on the 2nd April 1887. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.Th 30 and Hk.Tts. 80 per picul) and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, com&not; menced on that day. The collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, commenced on the 1st July 1887. The stations at present controlled by the Lappa Customs are known as the Malowchow (.1 &bull;&laquo; &raquo; and Chienshan (glf Hi) stations, with three sub-stations, known as the Shekkok (^3 ^j), Kuttai (&quot;pf fc), and Kwanchiap (&sect;j&sect; ppj) sub-stations. c. The word &quot; Inwards &quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II. MOVEMENTS OF VESSELS.&mdash;The number and movements of vessels, inwards and outwards during the quarter, are set forth in the following Tables :&mdash; i&deg;. MALOWCHOW. JUNKS. July. August.
